# odh-cli: Agent Context

[Overview](index.md) · [Agent Context](agent-context.md) · [Extracted Data](details.md) · [Network](network.md) · [RBAC](rbac.md) · [Security](security.md) · [Dataflow](dataflow.md)

Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- odh-cli is a Kubernetes component.
- with 1397 operator config constants (14 image references, 2 env vars).
- integrates with KServe, Kueue, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys OAuth proxy sidecar for authentication.
- references components\: data-science-pipelines (import, provider), kserve (import, provider), kueue (import, provider), llama-stack (import, provider), model-registry (import, provider), notebooks (import), opendatahub-operator (import), trainer (import), training-operator (import, provider).
